directly.\n\n## Asking another model for a review (e.g. \"Ask GLM 5.2 for a review\")\n\nThere is no subagent/model-routing tool. \"Asking\" another model means: fill the\nsuperpowers review template, then shell out to a nested `pi` in print mode.\n\n1. Read the `requesting-code-review` skill — its `SKILL.md` (how to drive the\n   review) and `code-reviewer.md` (the reviewer prompt template) — under\n   `~/.pi/agent/git/github.com/obra/superpowers/skills/requesting-code-review/`.\n\n2. Fill `code-reviewer.md`'s placeholders: DESCRIPTION (what was implemented),\n   PLAN_OR_REQUIREMENTS (the plan/requirements), BASE_SHA and HEAD_SHA (the\n   diff range). Write the filled prompt to `/tmp/review-prompt.md`.\n\n3. Send it to the reviewer:\n\n   ```bash\n   pi -p --no-session --no-extensions --no-skills -nc \\\n     --provider zai --model glm-5.2 \\\n     --thinking low --exclude-tools write,edit \\\n     \"$(cat /tmp/review-prompt.md)\"\n   ```\n\n   - GLM reviews run first-party at Z.ai: default `--provider zai --model glm-5.2`;\n     swap the model to `glm-5.3` when a heavier reviewer is wanted. Kimi K3 →\n     `--provider openrouter-curated --model moonshotai/kimi-k3`. OpenRouter\n     (`openrouter-curated`) carries no GLM models (vendor split, 2025-09-04); see\n     `~/.pi/agent/models.json` for the curated list.\n   - `--no-skills -nc` is safe: the review methodology travels in the prompt\n     (`code-reviewer.md`), not in the reviewer's loaded skills.
